# Master Testing Manifest

_Generated by `CANON/manifest.py` from `CANON/manifest.json`; never edited by hand. Every test of the project, numbered. A test is never removed: when it runs, it gets a dated result. Ask for any test by its number._

**How this list was built.** Built 2026-10-13 from six sub-agents combing the repository and the author's two pasted files. Their declared limits apply to every row: the development log from 2026-10-08 was read by heading and keyword, not in full; door notes were read selectively; data-set run outcomes were built mechanically from the registers and are provisional; some numbers in notes come from the pasted notes and are labeled there; class-era VAL results were not opened (author's blinding rule). Rows were merged only when they name the same test id; other likely duplicates are flagged 'possible duplicate of'.

**913 tests** · 433 with at least one run · 474 open · sections: [COM-METAA](#com-metaa), [COM-CSCORE](#com-cscore), [COM-IAMA](#com-iama), [ATL](#atl), [CAN](#can), [LEV](#lev), [PHY](#phy), [GRP](#grp), [TIS](#tis), [BRE](#bre), [FLD](#fld), [HYP](#hyp), [DAT](#dat), [SCO](#sco), [OTH](#oth), [HIS](#his)

Columns: **#** the test number · **Test** · **What it is for, and why it matters** · **Instrument / term** · **Data** (sources, and whether we have them) · **Status** · **Runs** (date, result) · **Next** · **Cost** (laptop, box, search) · **Impact** (5 = most) · **Record** · **Notes** (possible duplicates, undated reads, records not found).

## COM-CSCORE

**Commissioning: the clustering meter (C-score).** Reads the shape of loss, not its size; its band, held-out laboratories and repeatability checks.

## ATL

**The atlas.** The healthy reference profiles of every cell type that the readings are measured against: v2 history, the v2.1 build and its checks.

It matters because most Met-A expansions need the atlas to supply the healthy reference first. | IAM-Atlas v2 (U1-U5) | - · *partial* | run | **2026-10-12** PROGRESSED: Atlas commissioning plan written, use by use (draft; no test data read).<br>**2026-10-16** SUCCESS: atlas v2.1 commissioned use by use: U1 for three 450K cell references, U2a for the colon and lung blind sites; U3 failed; record doors/COMMISSIONING_NOTE_ATLAS_V21.md | Bars on independent purified cells for U2 first (TODO AT3), then U3, U4. | laptop | 5 | [COMMISSIONING_NOTE_ATLAS_V21](Biological_Physics/MethylPhys/doors/COMMISSIONING_NOTE_ATLAS_V21.md) | repo: Biological_Physics/MethylPhys/doors/DEV_ATLAS_COMMISSION_01.md; archive/retired_root_2026-10-19/TODO_to_2026-10-16.md AT3, AT5, AT6; next_jobs job 'AT3, AT5, AT6'. ## LEV

**Physics levers.** Handles on the methylation channel with predictions written before any data: tests of IAM itself while IAM-A is held.

## PHY

**Physics and derivation.** The derived marks of the gauge, the holding energy, the writer and eraser, and the open problem of deriving the middle.

Whiteboard arithmetic (post hoc, not a test): (A) loss-rate split writer:eraser 1:1 gives ln D - ln 2 = 3.17-3.69 kT (contains 3.41); virial 2:1 and 1:2 rate splits do not (2.76-3.28, 3.45-3.98). (B) energy split, a quarter of the SAM driving (amine proxy 12.2 kT + ln SAM/SAH 4-10) = 3.40-3.63 kT. Separating predictions: A, TET loss drops eps to ~0.0155, independent of methyl supply; B, E_hold = (dG + ln SAM/SAH)/4, halving SAM/SAH lowers it 0.17 kT (eps ~0.038). Before sealing: the C5-cytosine transfer free energy, and the author's mapping of potential/kinetic and dark energy/dark matter onto writer/eraser, quoted from the book.; author 2026-10-14: 'SAM writes, but something else all together erases.' Writing: DNMT1/3 on SAM (methionine, folate, B12). Erasing: TET1/2/3 on O2 and 2-oxoglutarate with Fe(II) and vitamin C (5mC to 5hmC, 5fC, 5caC), then TDG and base repair. Equal in amount, different in form, like the book's K and Q. Separate supplies give separate lever families: write side (methyl supply, SAM/SAH, DNMT inhibitors); erase side (oxygen and hypoxia, 2-oxoglutarate supply, vitamin C, TET blocked by 2-HG in IDH-mutant tumors and by succinate or fumarate in SDH or FH loss). |

Its check (bit_ledger_output.txt): dark energy would be exactly half the energy inside the horizon; measured 0 at the CMB, 0.4999 at z 0.298, 0.6861 today. Holds today only if dark energy is the half plus a further baseline (about 0.19 of today's total; the book's 'vacuum baseline', not derived) or the other half also went elsewhere. Irreversibility as the half spent into expansion: interpretation. / Significance (Planck 2018 Omega_Lambda 0.6847 +/- 0.0073, Omega_m 0.3153 +/- 0.0073): dark energy as half the total predicts 0.50, measured 0.6847, 25 sigma off (ruled out as stated); the assistant's own check, not the author's idea and not in the book: dark energy twice matter (from 2K + V = 0) predicts 2.00, measured 2.17 +/- 0.07, 2.3 sigma (Omega_Lambda = 1 - Omega_m in flat LCDM, so the error is propagated from Omega_m alone; an earlier 3.1 sigma treated the two as independent). Correction: DE = 0.4999 at z 0.298 is the matter-dark-energy equality, half by definition, not evidence. / The bill (author question): every horizon bit from the Big Bang to now, each paid at the horizon temperature when written, totals 1.66e70 J, exactly twice the energy inside the horizon today (8.31e69 J). Exact for any expansion history (the sum is c^5/(G H0)), so it follows from the horizon picture and is not a measurement. / 2026-10-16 book fixes (author: book errors MUST be told and fixed): glossary f_coll entry now matches the virial chapter (0.64 Sheth-Tormen, 0.71 Tinker extrapolated, 0.486 Tinker in range); p7_05b: R(1) = 2 stated as true by construction, not evidence, and the coincidence section retitled and marked an interpretation. The virial chapter (p2_02, p2_03, App. C3) already stated eta_vir is a definition and 0.62 lies below Tinker's range. / Book wording pass 2026-10-16 (author: 'I never use that language in public facing document'): draft overclaim phrases reworded in p3_01b (paradox), p7_02 (inertial objection), p7_05b (coincidence); the four phrases are retired in CANON/foundation.json so the gate refuses them. |

## FLD

**The field.** Whether tissue around a tumor, or the whole organ, is already off the healthy middle.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## HIS

**History: the class-era and early-chain process tests.** Every pre-registration and outcome from the class era and chains v1-v2 (PROC records), kept so no test is lost. Their results are not re-read here: the class-era VAL results stay blinded until current testing is done (TODO V1).
